The voltage drop across the inductor is given by The voltage drop across the resistor is given by The voltage drop across the capacitor is given by From Kirchoff’s Voltage Law, The voltage supplied = The voltage drop across the by the battery circuit components (the sum of these voltage drops is equal to the electromotive force ) EV0000716 · EV0000717 · EV0000718

Known gaps, conflicts and corrections

  1. 1 video-to-slide associations were not promoted to a match. Candidate rankings are listed in the transcript corpus and video-slide-matches.csv; the slide decks appear separately.
  2. 2 transcript segments have low ASR confidence; they are flagged with timestamps in the transcript blocks and indexed in the corpus.
  3. This is a visibly labelled source-preservation draft. Human page-level semantic and visual review is not complete; no unsupported subject summary or formula correction is claimed.
  4. The 19.26 GiB MP4 files remain hosted on Hugging Face; the full video path/hash and transcript coverage are indexed. The full audio/video was not watched end-to-end.
  5. Formula candidates are machine-extracted text with source locators; use the adjacent original slide page and the formula index before treating a candidate as an authoritative equation.
